# Development-seed tuning report — BROKEN_CATALYST_V1, CLEANING_CREW_V1, FIRST_DISH_V1, FOOD_TRAIL_V1, FUNGAL_SUPPLY_LINE_V1, HIDDEN_NEIGHBORHOOD_V1, L301_TWO_LUNCHES, L303_PUBLIC_KITCHEN, L304_EMPTY_AFTER_THE_FEAST, L306_BORROWED_SHELTER, LIGHT_AND_LIFE_V1, OIL_NEIGHBORHOOD_V1, ONE_HOST_V1, PREDATOR_BALANCE_V1, PROTEIN_CHAIN_V1, RESERVE_COMPARE_V1, SHARED_LUNCH_V1, STARCH_UNLOCK_V1 (standard)

Generated by `npx tsx tools/sim-tune.ts --recipes BROKEN_CATALYST_V1,CLEANING_CREW_V1,FIRST_DISH_V1,FOOD_TRAIL_V1,FUNGAL_SUPPLY_LINE_V1,HIDDEN_NEIGHBORHOOD_V1,L301_TWO_LUNCHES,L303_PUBLIC_KITCHEN,L304_EMPTY_AFTER_THE_FEAST,L306_BORROWED_SHELTER,LIGHT_AND_LIFE_V1,OIL_NEIGHBORHOOD_V1,ONE_HOST_V1,PREDATOR_BALANCE_V1,PROTEIN_CHAIN_V1,RESERVE_COMPARE_V1,SHARED_LUNCH_V1,STARCH_UNLOCK_V1 --seconds 600 --extend 1200 --presets standard --out docs/reports/tune-rc1.md` (tools/sim-tune.ts, tasks P1.12 and P2.9; CONTENT_TABLES §11; D06 §18).

- Content hash `d3fbd36c4a5e03468389b02508f2a3ecc1ce8d04b25cba5dd5e5be18f184a8bf`; rule versions (content/manifest.json): simulation 3, evolution rules 1, module registry 2, phenotype mapping 1, content 2; world schema 4. Mutation preset **standard**, no interventions.
- Build: git `c001a940250e1413f05de9eb21fce82699b27e6c` (HEAD when generated). src/sim/ and content/, everything the simulated numbers depend on, are identical to that commit.
- Stopping rule: run to 600 s; if no inherited difference (zero mutation events) by then, continue to 1200 s, keeping the 600 s checkpoint.
- Machine: linux x64 node v24.21.0, 2 CPU(s). Generated 2026-10-13T05:57:58.461Z. Total wall time 1206.8 s (headless Node, single thread; not the browser worker); 1-min load average 5.18 at start, 5.02 at end.
- Time convention: a milestone at tick *t* happened while simulating tick *t* (seconds shown = t / 10). "Within 15 s" means t < 150; "within 120 s" means t < 1200.
- Censoring: "none by T s" means no event before the stopping time. It is never counted as an event at T. Medians rank censored seeds above every observed value; when the median rank lands on a censored seed only a lower bound is given.
- Deaths by cause are tallied from `world.lineage.deathCause` over retained birth records (the event ring keeps only the last 500 events). The coverage column compares that tally with the total death-event counter; they differ only if lineage records were compacted.
- First intake per species is observed read-only after stage 6 each tick from the feeding flag (set exactly when an organism took carbon that tick); the world milestone `firstIntake` covers any species, photosynthesis included.
- Daughter trials and module draws are observed after every tick from the new birth records (so lineage compaction cannot lose them). A module draw that finds no legal option changes nothing and leaves no trace in the genome, so each committed daughter's draw is recomputed from its recorded keys (seed, parent birth id, daughter index) with the simulation's pure `draftDaughter`, probing a module set with a legal gain and one with a legal loss. "Draws reproduced" re-drafts every daughter from its parent's recorded genome and compares the genome and mutation descriptor with the record; "mismatches" counts recorded module changes that disagree with the recomputed draw (both must be clean). D06 §9 calls a module draw that chose gain a *gain attempt*; module events are dated by the daughter's birth tick.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

No daughter was a trial for any module by 600 s.

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

No organism carried a module on any seed by 600 s.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

| Seed | Daughters (trials) | Module draws: gain / loss | Gains (module) | Gain draws with no legal option | Losses | Loss draws with nothing to lose | Gains by species | Carriers alive (module: species) | Gains with a living carrier | First gain | Draws reproduced | Mismatches |

#### Per-daughter rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

| Module | Gain trials (by species) | Gains | Per gain trial | Expected gains (95 % range) | Gains per simulated minute (expected) | Loss trials (by species) | Losses | Per loss trial | Expected losses (95 % range) | Losses per simulated minute (expected) |

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

| Seed | Daughters by 600 s | Chance of no gain attempt by 600 s | Gain-eligible daughters by 600 s | Chance of no committed gain by 600 s | Median wait for a committed gain (s) | First gain attempt | First gain |

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

No organism carried a module on any seed by 600 s.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

Effective speed = 0.1 s of simulated time ÷ mean wall time per tick (how many times faster than real time this machine could step the dish, with no rendering). "Plain run" re-runs the seed with no observer and no hooks (realizeRecipe, then step) and compares its state hash with this run's: equal hashes show the tuner only observed.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

| Seed | Daughters (trials) | Module draws: gain / loss | Gains (module) | Gain draws with no legal option | Losses | Loss draws with nothing to lose | Gains by species | Carriers alive (module: species) | Gains with a living carrier | First gain | Draws reproduced | Mismatches |

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

| Seed | Daughters by 600 s | Chance of no gain attempt by 600 s | Gain-eligible daughters by 600 s | Chance of no committed gain by 600 s | Median wait for a committed gain (s) | First gain attempt | First gain |

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

The secretion stage checks energy before substrate (src/sim/structures.ts), so a recorded outcome names only the first condition that failed; the last three columns measure both conditions directly at the end of each second: deposited starch in or beside the carrier's cell (the stage's own substrate test) and energy at or below the carrier's recorded release threshold.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

| Seed | Branch | Difference from its ancestor | Variation observed at | Confirmed at | Members at confirmation (generations) | Alive at 600 s (with sub-branches) | Peak | Extinct at |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 155921 | Sunbead · Light seeker · 7A7 | gained E07 | 252.9 s | 433.2 s | 5 (4) | 9 (9) | 9 | — |

Effective speed = 0.1 s of simulated time ÷ mean wall time per tick (how many times faster than real time this machine could step the dish, with no rendering). "Plain run" re-runs the seed with no observer and no hooks (realizeRecipe, then step) and compares its state hash with this run's: equal hashes show the tuner only observed.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

| Seed | Daughters by 600 s | Chance of no gain attempt by 600 s | Gain-eligible daughters by 600 s | Chance of no committed gain by 600 s | Median wait for a committed gain (s) | First gain attempt | First gain |

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

No organism carried a module on any seed by 600 s.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

Effective speed = 0.1 s of simulated time ÷ mean wall time per tick (how many times faster than real time this machine could step the dish, with no rendering). "Plain run" re-runs the seed with no observer and no hooks (realizeRecipe, then step) and compares its state hash with this run's: equal hashes show the tuner only observed.

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

No daughter was a trial for any module by 600 s.

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

No organism carried a module on any seed by 600 s.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

Effective speed = 0.1 s of simulated time ÷ mean wall time per tick (how many times faster than real time this machine could step the dish, with no rendering). "Plain run" re-runs the seed with no observer and no hooks (realizeRecipe, then step) and compares its state hash with this run's: equal hashes show the tuner only observed.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

| Seed | Daughters (trials) | Module draws: gain / loss | Gains (module) | Gain draws with no legal option | Losses | Loss draws with nothing to lose | Gains by species | Carriers alive (module: species) | Gains with a living carrier | First gain | Draws reproduced | Mismatches |

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

| Seed | Daughters by 600 s | Chance of no gain attempt by 600 s | Gain-eligible daughters by 600 s | Chance of no committed gain by 600 s | Median wait for a committed gain (s) | First gain attempt | First gain |

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

No organism carried a module on any seed by 600 s.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

#### Per-daughter rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

By species (pooled): .

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

No daughter was a trial for any module by 600 s.

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

| Seed | Daughters (trials) | Module draws: gain / loss | Gains (module) | Gain draws with no legal option | Losses | Loss draws with nothing to lose | Gains by species | Carriers alive (module: species) | Gains with a living carrier | First gain | Draws reproduced | Mismatches |

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

Effective speed = 0.1 s of simulated time ÷ mean wall time per tick (how many times faster than real time this machine could step the dish, with no rendering). "Plain run" re-runs the seed with no observer and no hooks (realizeRecipe, then step) and compares its state hash with this run's: equal hashes show the tuner only observed.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

Effective speed = 0.1 s of simulated time ÷ mean wall time per tick (how many times faster than real time this machine could step the dish, with no rendering). "Plain run" re-runs the seed with no observer and no hooks (realizeRecipe, then step) and compares its state hash with this run's: equal hashes show the tuner only observed.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

By species (pooled): .

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

No daughter was a trial for any module by 600 s.

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

| Seed | Daughters (trials) | Module draws: gain / loss | Gains (module) | Gain draws with no legal option | Losses | Loss draws with nothing to lose | Gains by species | Carriers alive (module: species) | Gains with a living carrier | First gain | Draws reproduced | Mismatches |

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

No organism carried a module on any seed by 600 s.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

"Plain run" re-runs the seed with no observer and no hooks (realizeRecipe, then step) and compares its state hash with this run's: equal hashes show the tuner only observed.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

| Seed | Daughters (trials) | Module draws: gain / loss | Gains (module) | Gain draws with no legal option | Losses | Loss draws with nothing to lose | Gains by species | Carriers alive (module: species) | Gains with a living carrier | First gain | Draws reproduced | Mismatches |

#### Per-daughter rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

A small batch characterizes this prototype dish; it does not establish population-wide probabilities (D06 §18). "Per simulated minute" is the pooled count ÷ 60.0 dish-minutes (mean per dish per minute).

| Gain attempt | 0 | 0 | — | 0.1 % | — | 0.000 |
| Gain committed | 0 | 0 | — | — | — (needs a legal option) | 0.000 |
| Loss attempt | 0 | 0 | — | 0.1 % | — | 0.000 |

By species (pooled): .

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

No daughter was a trial for any module by 600 s.

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

| Seed | Daughters by 600 s | Chance of no gain attempt by 600 s | Gain-eligible daughters by 600 s | Chance of no committed gain by 600 s | Median wait for a committed gain (s) | First gain attempt | First gain |

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

No organism carried a module on any seed by 600 s.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

No daughter was a trial for any module by 600 s.

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

| Seed | Daughters by 600 s | Chance of no gain attempt by 600 s | Gain-eligible daughters by 600 s | Chance of no committed gain by 600 s | Median wait for a committed gain (s) | First gain attempt | First gain |

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

No organism carried a module on any seed by 600 s.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

"Plain run" re-runs the seed with no observer and no hooks (realizeRecipe, then step) and compares its state hash with this run's: equal hashes show the tuner only observed.

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

### Module draws per daughter trial at the 600 s checkpoint

Each division commits two daughters; each daughter is one trial of every draw. A module draw picks gain or loss with equal chance, then a legal option; with no legal option nothing changes (for example a loss drawn by an organism that carries no module). "Carriers alive" counts living organisms carrying each module; "gains with a living carrier" counts committed gains that still have at least one living descendant carrying the module.

| Seed | Daughters (trials) | Module draws: gain / loss | Gains (module) | Gain draws with no legal option | Losses | Loss draws with nothing to lose | Gains by species | Carriers alive (module: species) | Gains with a living carrier | First gain | Draws reproduced | Mismatches |

#### Per-module rates, pooled over 6 seeds (600 s each)

D06 §18: for rare modules, events per daughter trial as well as per minute. A daughter is a trial for a module's gain when its parent could legally gain that module (eligible species, a free slot, nothing excluding it); a gain draw picks uniformly among the parent's legal gains, so its chance for one module is the gain-attempt chance ÷ the parent's number of legal gains (three for a Sprinter with no module, one for a Sunbead). A daughter is a trial for a module's loss when its parent carried it. "Expected" sums those chances over the trials (95 % range: Poisson). Per simulated minute = count ÷ dish-minutes, observed (expected).

No daughter was a trial for any module by 600 s.

#### Waiting time for a module gain (Standard; gain attempt 0.1 % per daughter)

A gain attempt can fall on any daughter; it commits a gain only when the parent has a legal gain (a gain-eligible daughter). Computed from the preset chance and each dish's own daughters, not from the observed gains: the chance that no daughter drew a gain attempt (all daughters), the chance that no gain was committed (gain-eligible daughters), and the second by which the latter first fell to one half (the median wait for a committed gain at this dish's daughter production). The last two columns are what happened.

| Seed | Daughters by 600 s | Chance of no gain attempt by 600 s | Gain-eligible daughters by 600 s | Chance of no committed gain by 600 s | Median wait for a committed gain (s) | First gain attempt | First gain |

#### Module carriers, pooled over 6 seeds

Carrier-seconds are carriers counted at the end of every simulated second up to 600 s. "Extra room in use" = an E05 carrier with energy above its base cap (reserve fill band 1–3); "not active" = an E03 carrier preparing, resting or waking; "secreting" = an E01 carrier releasing enzyme. Energy columns compare living carriers at 600 s with the same species' organisms that carry no module.

No organism carried a module on any seed by 600 s.

### Branch confirmations by 600 s

A candidate is confirmed as a branch when at least 5 qualifying members are alive and one lives at least 3 generations beyond its root (SPEC §8.5); a single mutation is never a branch. The name is the generated name a player sees. "Alive" counts own members, then members including branches descended from it.

No branch was confirmed on any seed by 600 s.

## Observations

Written by the lead at the v1.0 gate (2026-10-13). The run used the same command, recipes, seeds and preset as
tune-g3.md, at `c001a94` (header), on the shared 2-CPU machine while `npm run check` ran (load ≈ 5).

- **Every generated table equals tune-g3.md's.** Two kinds of value must move, and are masked for the comparison:
  - the state hashes, because they cover the contentHash, which D-0072's buildPhase 3 → 4 changed;
  - the two wall-clock columns (ms per tick and the speed-up), because they depend on the machine's load.

  Everything else is identical on all 108 runs (18 recipes × 6 seeds): births, deaths, living counts, peaks, food
  totals, conversions, first intake and division times, mutation and module-draw counts, branches, deaths by cause,
  ledger errors, and "draws reproduced" with 0 mismatches. The comparison:
  `diff <(sed 1,6d tune-g3.md | mask) <(sed 1,6d tune-rc1.md | mask)`, where `mask` rewrites every backticked
  16-hex hash and every "| a.aaa (b.bbb) | c.c× |" timing pair. It leaves only the Machine line and the two
  hand-written sections.
- This is the seed-level evidence for D-0072: Phase 4 changed no simulated outcome. G3's observations and owner
  questions (tune-g3.md "Observations": producers starve in the enzyme dishes, D-0022 and D-0051; curated dishes that
  end empty, EXPANSION_RESPONSE §8) stand unchanged and are not repeated here.

## Proposed recipe revisions

None at v1.0. No recipe changed in Phase 4. The open questions are the owner items carried from G3.
